# Build Plan Implementation and Qualification History

These excerpts preserve implementation decisions, candidate results and
qualification history moved out of [BUILD_PLAN.md](../BUILD_PLAN.md) when its
completed milestones were consolidated into scope records. They were copied
from the plan based on commit `12dc11e34bd4c77964bc68b546acdd534973f757`;
the concurrent Milestone 27d activation is outside these excerpts. Each excerpt
includes the SHA-256 of its original UTF-8 text. Only trailing line whitespace
is normalized in the literal copies below; wording and record order are retained.

The excerpts retain their original chronology, task numbering, paths, source and
target limits, failed attempts, and exceptions. Relative paths inside the literal
blocks retain their original BUILD_PLAN context. Superseded candidate contracts
and outstanding checks describe their original state; they do not activate work,
override selected generated truth or qualify a later image. Current milestone
scope and status remain in the build plan. Release waivers, retirement decisions
and evidence carry-forward remain governed by their canonical audit records.

References to removed `M26C_*` documents in these literal excerpts resolve to
the [pinned audit archive](https://github.com/lukeb-aidev/cohesix/tree/9bffe4c860bacb6946e2d9d1e9d032537d4aca29/docs/audit).

- [26b qualification](#milestone-26b-summary) and [CYW43 closure](#m26b-wifi-sdio-notification-dpc-closure-changes).
- [26c scope and evidence correction](#milestone-26c-summary).
- [26d baseline](#milestone-26d-summary) and [hardware-free CYW43 work](#m26d-cyw43-hardware-free-closure-changes).
- [26e scope](#milestone-26e-summary), [drivers](#m26e-driver-runtime-mcs-port-and-cyw43-coexistence-changes), [console/network](#m26e-console-network-service-isolation-changes), [checks](#m26e-console-network-service-isolation-checks), [performance](#m26e-qemu-shared-control-path-performance-changes) and [QEMU result](#m26e-qemu-performance-result).
- [27c agent-adoption validation](#m27c-agent-adoption-validation).

## milestone-26b-summary

Excerpt SHA-256: `88440989fd4fc1108b91ce6bddf25817a66046663a5a61138fdfc933a699b718`.

````text
**Status:** Complete — exact image `7a10b8fd6accc3d47ab4726cdd3de5828042c9f3` restored the previously reliable USB enumeration/command-ready path and passed the operator-approved current-image Wi-Fi and same-image GENET closure. The five requested Wi-Fi reboot attempts were completed; W01-W03 and W05 passed, while W04 is retained honestly as an operator-excluded RF-affected sample rather than counted as a passing lifetime. The accepted cold, W01-W03, and W05 Wi-Fi samples had no semantic errors, and the GENET control did not regress. No fresh USB key was typed during this matrix. The operator explicitly accepted current Gate 10, the exact one-deep idle-report queue, command readiness, zero current USB recovery/no-reply debt, and healthy HDMI completions together with exact restoration of the previously board-proven `2668c34f76ff` path as sufficient final local-seat evidence; this record does not claim a fresh first-byte or printable-key observation.

The **2026-08-11 accepted matrix** uses source `7a10b8fd6acc`: cold Wi-Fi, W01–W03 and W05 passed all fifteen early scripts and 16,446/16,446 no-retry pressure operations. Every tested lifetime completed raw64 and reached DHCP/USB Gate 10 with healthy HDMI; W01–W05 and GENET G01 also recorded the one-deep idle-report queue. W04 completed its scripts and every wire-command terminal but failed the REST error budget (25/757 timeouts, 3.3025%) amid RF/ingress delay; it remains operator-excluded, never a passing lifetime. GENET G01 passed 9,213/9,213 pressure operations and raw TCP at 180.269 requests/s with 1.677-ms p95, without wired regression.

The **2026-08-10 historical baseline**, source `2668c34f76ff`, passed one cold plus five rebooted Wi-Fi lifetimes and a same-image GENET control. Wi-Fi passed all 18 scripts and 18,363 pressure operations with no semantic errors; recovered retransmissions remained distinct from loss, reconnects and driver failures. GENET passed all scripts, raw TCP at 178.082 requests/s with 1.874-ms p95, and 9,711/9,712 REST operations; its one typed buffer-full refusal stayed within the accepted 1% budget. The operator accepted this research/diagnostic Wi-Fi envelope and unchanged wired control in place of the earlier 10-cold/10-warm, <=40-ms p95 and >=29-requests/s proposal. Semantic error accounting was unchanged. The host-only prompt-tail reboot repair passed 79 focused tests and added no target authority.

[Complete boot matrices and immutable provenance](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/lukeb-aidev/cohesix/fa55f5f18c0724ab8724f0d28defaf0dda5ce1b0/docs/BUILD_PLAN.md) retain both sources' build/image/manifest identities, every serial and paired-pcap SHA-256, per-lifetime latency/throughput and excluded samples. Capture hashes cover complete-record prefixes; live-writer tails were excluded. G01's 4,345 wire commands differ from its 9,213 REST operations; the excluded final FIN is supplemented by socket/process absence and zero reconnects. These historical records do not qualify a changed image.

**Reopened defect history and built result:** The accepted DHCP, USB/local-seat, QEMU compatibility and wired GENET baseline was retained. Investigation during 26d restored the original 26b root-no-wait contract through the task definitions below:

- `m26b-wifi-sdio-notification-dpc-closure` established generated IRQ/notification topology, sequence-last reciprocal DPC/RX records, bounded isolated owner service, Linux-aligned enable/ALP timing, persistent bootstrap supervision and whole-action pair restart. Persistent op11, finite urgent-op7 and event-bound DPC leases use durable conditions; coalesced notifications supply no authority. Private phases progress to bounded quiescence and named child/credit/queue/peer waits block immediately. Missing/competing intake seals produce typed zero-I/O terminals.
- Control/data restoration keeps TX submission queue-only, retires each issued op7 before fresh promotion, and prioritizes committed RX. A joined Function-2 terminal releases the root parent; SDPCM tx_max remains runtime admission, not a second root acknowledgement. Reserved response space and current-generation causal PTK successors survive bounded rotation without another issuer.
- Join/control restoration separates logical connection generations from physical bus epochs, opens and retains the outer Join lease across named waits, and makes DPC activation generation-long. Healthy owner-active state avoids redundant activation; committed events and exact ACK debt retain their existing authority. Function-2 control/Join retains its physical DPC fence, terminal IRQ closure and durable empty-ring rearm.
- EAPOL M2/M4/group-key replies use the sealed finite four-operation parent. The disproven EAPOL-only pre-TX fence was removed; pending CARD_INT does not reject an already-sealed data write. ACK_PENDING without a committed event is a publication window, not DPC authority. Generation-bound RX poison reaches the existing restart supervisor; the passive recovery-source-line record adds observation only.
- `m26b-usb-hdmi-local-seat-liveness` added counter-scaled held-arrow repeat and current-lifetime prompt/banner/display admission. Follow-up proof bookkeeping regressed enumeration at phases 198/316/412; runtime OldgoodReceipt publication and the redundant descriptor-proof scheduler were removed. The reserved ABI slot and zero-record projection remain passive. The board-proven `2668c34f76ff` enumeration/one-deep command path was restored, with PCIe/USB preparation in established attach order and one owner registration before enumeration. HDMI output/prompt progress is independent of Wi-Fi readiness; USB parser admission remains separate.

**Historical failure sequence:** `f9ba51abf` stopped after control TX before reply RX; `9e673510bf43` at redundant Join activation; `51741e5c25d9` at logical/physical generation comparison; `a9c01a58f2c0` at issued M2 before terminal; `5fa7c3871d96` at M4 or delivery of captured DHCP replies; `514036b706f5` at committed RX poison/PTK-successor/display progress; `325756c800b0` lacked the first recovery call-site identity; `5b57ebcdcf73` retained Join without command publication. These failed/partial runs are retained in the [immutable chronology](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/lukeb-aidev/cohesix/fa55f5f18c0724ab8724f0d28defaf0dda5ce1b0/docs/BUILD_PLAN.md); the later accepted matrix supersedes their pending-proof status without relabeling them. The earlier `78d5195582c7` 5/5 trace remains a historical oracle; its recurrent Poll/Grant cadence was not restored.

The accepted `7a10b8fd6acc` closure explicitly records `physical_input_proven=no`: no fresh key was typed. Repeated Gate 10, one-deep idle-report, command-ready, recovery-free and HDMI-complete sentinels plus exact restoration of the board-proven path were owner-accepted for this image only. Future changed images retain the normal physical-input and owner/descriptor proof requirements. The repairs added no operator protocol, namespace, second owner, poller, fallback or production Wi-Fi parity claim.

**Why now (operator continuity):**
Milestone 26b depends on completed 26a driver-task substrate and wired/serial/display isolation. Milestone 26b applies that model to the two paths that exposed the regression: USB keyboard/local-seat and CYW43 Wi-Fi. Wi-Fi and selected-network performance must improve by moving SDIO/firmware/RX/TX or GENET RX/TX progress onto bounded manifest-declared isolated driver runtimes, not by extending the root event-loop turn.

**Non-negotiable constraints:**
- DHCP implementation must be pure Rust, `no_std`, and intentionally bare-bones (DHCPv4 only: DISCOVER/OFFER/REQUEST/ACK plus bounded timeout/retry logic).
- No new in-VM listeners or protocols are permitted; DHCP is only a client-side address acquisition path for existing network surfaces.
- Post-seL4 runtime must remain HAL-only; no direct bootloader/firmware service calls are allowed in root-task.
- NIC and Wi-Fi DHCP behavior must be policy-configurable for `pi4-uboot-aarch64` through compiler-validated profile settings sourced from U-Boot environment configuration when available; if bootloader policy inputs are absent, the Pi 4 manifest defaults are authoritative and select DHCP/`auto` so GENET DHCP is the no-credential path and Wi-Fi DHCP is selected by explicit Wi-Fi policy or credentials.
- Wi-Fi scope is minimal diagnostics connectivity only (join + DHCP + existing TCP console path); no in-VM supplicant stack, roaming framework, or broad feature surface.
- Wi-Fi is a research, diagnostics, and degraded-link bring-up transport. Production Pi 4 deployments that require high worker concurrency or predictable REST latency should use the wired GENET path unless a site-specific Wi-Fi envelope is freshly proven and documented.
- Milestone 26b includes a new profile-gated Pi 4 CYW43xx Wi-Fi driver path; all Wi-Fi dataplane/control-plane access must be HAL-backed (SDIO, power/reset, IRQ/OOB, firmware handoff hooks) with no direct MMIO or firmware-service calls outside HAL.
- CYW43xx design references must be used in this order for architecture review: OpenBSD `bwfm` -> Zephyr/Infineon WHD HAL split -> Linux `brcmfmac` SDIO edge-case behavior. These are design references only; no source copy/paste is permitted.
- USB/xHCI/HID owns a higher-priority driver-task contract than Wi-Fi data. Keyboard first-byte and fast-typing proof are hard gates before Wi-Fi performance claims.
- CYW43/SDIO runs under separate network-control and network-data budgets. EAPOL, DHCP, ARP, and TCP ACK progress may preempt Wi-Fi bulk data, but neither class may preempt USB/local-seat or serial input.
- Runtime RX aggregation/glom may be enabled only after bounded superframe storage, capped deaggregation work, drop counters, and recovery gates are implemented inside the Wi-Fi driver task.
- Performance claims use normalized parity: raw `cohsh` over Pi Wi-Fi is measured first, REST gateway overhead is measured separately, QEMU remains a compatibility/capacity reference by itself, and production Pi throughput-parity claims must come from a fresh same-harness wired/GENET Pi run that meets or exceeds the selected best-QEMU throughput reference.
- Wi-Fi performance claims use the documented research envelope instead of QEMU parity: the safe sustained 26b Wi-Fi worker cap is `120` workers, short exploratory pressure may be run up to `300` workers, and `1500` workers is a fault-injection/stress ceiling only. Wi-Fi runs must pass the accepted error budget, preserve raw TCP/cohsh reachability, keep CYW43/SDIO counters free of drops/overflows/faults, and report latency separately as physical-link evidence.
- The isolated runtime throughput verdict excludes QEMU-latency parity only after recording latency fields in the artifacts and showing Pi 4 wired NIC and Wi-Fi latency is explained and aligned with the selected transport's documented physical-network expectations. Throughput, successful operation count, error rate, and bounded-backpressure behavior remain verdict inputs for production wired/GENET. Persistent second-scale stalls, unbounded queue growth, drops, or timeout-driven success remain blockers for production throughput claims even when exploratory Wi-Fi stress passes its error budget.
- Backward compatibility is mandatory: Milestone 26b must not break existing macOS/Linux QEMU workflows, fixtures, or transport semantics.

````

## milestone-26c-summary

Excerpt SHA-256: `13e5291551f9ce2ad44d8df7b94ffc6137269d2c69f10aef81723ab4db81d1ac`.

````text
**Why now (reviewer trust):**
Milestones 25-26b establish technical capability, transport breadth, Pi 4 bring-up evidence, and isolated runtime benchmark closure, but the implementation has accumulated visible scaffolding, duplicated validation paths, long runtime modules, and uneven characterization coverage. Milestone 26c is the aggressive refactor window after isolated runtime benchmark closure and before seL4 15 realignment: it inventories tracked Markdown authoring surfaces, records docs-as-built truth, expands characterization and boundary gates, and then permits broad behavior-preserving refactors across Cohesix-authored host tools, root-task adapters, HAL-facing network code, tests, and public documentation. Cleanup is complete only when the target-qualified staged Test Plan passes on both QEMU and Pi 4 with evidence that external behavior did not drift.

**Current planning status:** Complete.

The documentation-only task
`m26c-readme-linked-doc-suite-remediation`, including its preservation and
newcomer-glossary follow-ups, completed on 15 July 2026. The later correction
that removed fabricated LoRa-radio semantics was authorized and closed
separately under Milestone 18; it does not leave Milestone 26c open. Historical
release snapshots remain immutable, and their superseded wording is recorded
in `docs/audit/M26C_DOC_DRIFT_LEDGER.md`.

Milestone 26d reopened only the truth and evidence attached to the former
Worker-execution, endpoint-cap, notification, and applied-scheduling claims.
The helper loops and generated badge/scheduling records remain useful model
artifacts, but they did not establish a loaded Worker image, TCB, CSpace,
VSpace, delivered cap, or target invocation. The historical implementation
tasks below are inactive and retained for traceability only; they may not be
executed under this reopening. Actual Worker objects, endpoint authority,
notifications, or MCS scheduling require a future explicitly authorized atomic
task that also satisfies the 26d MCS and root-TCB decision gates. Milestone 26e
owns root-service and Worker task isolation plus the SMP+MCS architecture and
target acceptance; Milestone 27d validates mixed-load operator scheduling on
that accepted architecture, and Milestone 27e owns production Worker
ticket/lease ledger binding, complete driver-inventory projection, and
structured quarantine/evidence. Other accepted 26c QEMU/Pi evidence remains
historical and is not invalidated by this correction.

The original QEMU and Pi 4 closure remains accepted. QEMU closure is anchored
by `out/test-plan/m26c-qemu` and Stage 05 due-diligence root
`out/audit/gate/20260628T015332Z`. Pi 4 closure is anchored by the final wired
GENET boot in `/Users/lukasbower/pi4-serial-20260629-135454.log`, paired
USB-Ethernet capture `/Users/lukasbower/tcpdump-usb-eth-20260629-135504.pcap`,
runtime/DMA proof bundle
`out/test-plan/m26c-pi4-live/pi4-runtime-dma-proof-genet-latest.env`, direct TCP
proof `out/test-plan/m26c-pi4-live/cohsh-tcp-proof-genet-latest.txt`, and Stage
05 due-diligence root `out/audit/gate/20260629T061204Z`. That accepted proof is
wired GENET at `192.168.10.50` with `PI4_RUNTIME_DMA_PROOF=fresh-pi`,
`PI4_RUNTIME_DMA_COUNTER_PROOF=counter-qualified`,
`TIMER_BACKEND=arch-counter`, `TIMER_CLOCK_HZ=54000000`,
`TIMER_EL0_COUNTER=vct`, DHCP-bound TCP `cohsh`, and REST/gateway validation.
It does not substitute for current-image Milestone 26d Pi 4 revalidation.
````

## milestone-26d-summary

Excerpt SHA-256: `6c19efd7d12c59b739792c953ea672c0a4b9203e9339c2b85852661788a89a41`.

````text
**Why now (kernel truth):**
Milestone 26c makes the docs-as-built audit, target-qualified Test Plan, host/VM boundary evidence, and regression-gated refactor baseline explicit. Milestone 26d refreshes the external kernel baseline and canonical references to seL4 16.0.0, anchors manual alignment to the official seL4 Reference Manual v16.0.0 ([PDF](https://sel4.systems/Info/Docs/seL4-manual-16.0.0.pdf)), proves the reopened 26a/26b driver-task model still holds on QEMU and Pi 4, and closes kernel-version drift before later feature work builds on stale assumptions. Because a kernel refresh changes scheduler, syscall, timer, cache, and generated-artifact behavior that can move measured latency or throughput, 26d also owns a bounded benchmark revalidation and regression-tuning lane: compare historical-best, accepted 26b, accepted seL4 15, first seL4 16, and post-tuning seL4 16 evidence before later milestones rely on the refreshed baseline.

Milestone 26b was subsequently reopened for the steady-data-plane and
local-seat regressions and returned to Complete on exact image
`7a10b8fd6acc` through the operator-approved matrix recorded above. Its
`2668c34f76ff` transport matrix remains the performance/reliability baseline;
the current-image record is final milestone closure. Urgent-only
lease eligibility, bulk exclusion, normal deadline or
hintless scheduling, and zero/no-ack fallback language retained inside the
completed 26d task records below describes the superseded evidence baseline;
it is not the current runtime contract. The accepted Milestone 26b contract is
the `m26b-wifi-sdio-notification-dpc-closure` task above: every admitted
current-generation post-Gate-8 op7 uses the same finite cadence, provenance
affects only capacity and urgent-before-bulk ordering, child completion is
durable before wake and rechecked before sleep, software-only drains are
bounded to eight terminals per Network wake, and accepted ordinary or pressure
traffic uses no deadline, hintless-watchdog, fallback, or second-owner lane.

The seL4 16 capability audit also found three pre-existing assurance defects that
must close before the refreshed-baseline claim is credible: isolated runtime
mappings were not uniformly fail-closed for execute permission, QEMU kernel
intent was not reproducible from one pinned upstream manifest/profile contract,
and generated Worker declarations overstated target execution. Milestone 26d
owns the bounded security and truth repairs below; it does not use those repairs
to imply new product behavior or completed Pi 4 Wi-Fi evidence.

````

## milestone-26e-summary

Excerpt SHA-256: `e8852a4de13ee96de064a6e52c15fcf905042868e935738b5ab3e2f422bd87f8`.

````text
**Evidence record:** [Benchmark results and run references](BENCHMARKS.md), [release audit](audit/AUDIT_REPORT_2026-09-13.md), and the approved carry-forward/waiver above retain the qualification basis. The [immutable historical evidence archive](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/lukeb-aidev/cohesix/fa55f5f18c0724ab8724f0d28defaf0dda5ce1b0/docs/BUILD_PLAN.md) retains the full earlier source/image hashes, serial/packet identities, per-run measurements and failed experiments summarized below. Historical failures remain failures for their original source, target and gate; they are not current acceptance.

**Why now (userspace TCB reduction):** Milestone 26d establishes honest
seL4 16 kernel/profile truth and records that NineDoor parsing, TCP/smoltcp,
provider dispatch, policy, supervision, and Worker model state still execute in
the principal root-task authority domain. It also corrects the former claim
that general Workers already run as isolated target tasks. Once the refreshed
QEMU/Pi baseline and CYW43 evidence are stable, Cohesix can reduce that
userspace TCB in the security-first order recorded by
`docs/audit/M26D_ROOT_TCB_BOUNDARY_AUDIT.md` without mixing a system-model
change into hardware bring-up.

The same audit found a second trust-boundary problem that must close before the
split can be accepted. Selected code can currently reach dummy time, a null
serial backend, preseeded target `/host` state (`systemd=active`, NVIDIA
`ok`/`42C`), mock 4090/4060 GPU entries, a spinning target `nine-door` binary,
and bootstrap commands that answer with `stub`/`not implemented`. The host GPU
bridge can also replace a missing or invalid live model registry with fixture
models and an implicit active model. Those paths are useful only as explicit
tests or developer simulations. They are prohibited from the 26e operational
QEMU/Pi dependency closure and from all target, Worker, integration, release,
and use-case evidence.

MCS is the preferred Cohesix temporal-isolation architecture, not a feature
checkbox or a throughput shortcut. Capability-controlled CPU time, enforced
budget/period ceilings, timeout faults, explicit Reply objects, and consumed
time evidence match Cohesix's mixed-criticality shape: root/Queen policy,
emergency serial, and fault supervision must remain live beside untrusted
parsers, network ingress, Workers, drivers, telemetry, and display work. The
split must therefore establish the MCS task/IPC contract before child ABIs
freeze. seL4 16 still describes SMP+MCS as less explored and experimental, so
exact QEMU/Pi acceptance is a hard evidence gate. Cohesix nevertheless selects
one architecture: accepted 26e operational profiles use SMP+MCS on both targets.
There is no compiled classic-SMP fallback; an unforeseen blocker requires
reverting the atomic 26e scheduler change set before another architecture can
be planned.

````

## m26b-wifi-sdio-notification-dpc-closure-changes

Excerpt SHA-256: `1c20c619b84e82d491fa7705000f4298100acab02620495630b04c486b6c8cda`.

````text
Changes:
  - Diagnostic compatibility note — this task intentionally versions the non-authority-bearing `wifi diag` accounting and truth telemetry with `owner_active=yes|no`, updates the trace normalizer and gate proof together, and keeps historical lines readable but non-acceptable without active-owner proof. It changes no authority-bearing console command, network protocol, namespace, or benchmark workload grammar; later references to no console/network protocol change use that narrower meaning.
  - Current DPC-client accounting repair — add one cache-isolated, exact 128-byte `DriverRuntimeCyw43DpcClientRecord` at shared offset 49,984 through 50,111 (`[49,984, 50,112)`), immediately after the DPC-child timing record and below the 53,248-byte CYW43-private RX-region end. CYW43 is the sole writer and publishes the current physical epoch, live consumer sequence, owner-rearm attempts, errors, cause counters, DPC turns, owner children/turns, and completed-frame counters at existing initialization, quiescent owner-rearm, and terminal-fault checkpoints by clearing the final commit word, writing and cleaning the body, then repeating a nonzero publication sequence last. Root stable-double-reads the record and accepts it only between identical stable reads of the live SDIO owner ring (`ring -> record -> ring`) with exact physical-epoch and live-consumer binding. A torn, stale, raced, or mismatched sample is rerun-required and acceptance-red, but grants no admission, notification, wake, issue, retry, rearm, recovery, deadline, scheduling, or physical-owner authority. The additive v11 completion trace remains historical/compatibility input, not current DPC-client truth.
  - Current Gate 7/Gate 8 diagnostic transaction — bracket each passive current `wifi diag` with a nonzero `wifi: diag_begin id=<id> pair_epoch=<pair> generation=<gen> snapshot=current` and matching `wifi: diag_complete id=<id> ... snapshot=current pair=<pair> gen=<gen> ...`. Emit the compact retained Gate 7 `7a>7b>7c>7d>7e` receipt only when its exact pair/generation matches that transaction; `src=sm` means current retained host-EAPOL state-machine proof, not generic Gate 8 inference. Bind every intervening Gate 8 subgate to the same pair/generation. The normalizer accepts retained or clipped proof only from one exact begin/rows/complete identity and fails closed on standalone, prior, reordered, malformed, scrubbed, or cross-identity rows; these records remain passive evidence only.
  - Current owner/old-good diagnostic closure — the exact `2668c34f76ff` matrix proved Gate 10 and a healthy current DPC ring while every post-settle serial slice still lacked durable CYW43/SDIO owner-state, firmware-identity, and ordered pre-association control receipts. Do not infer those prerequisites from Gate 7, Gate 8, DHCP, TCP, or a bootstrap-stage breadcrumb. Source compact owner rows only from current HAL owner registration plus descriptor-seal truth, and project one bounded retained old-good transaction only from the current supervisor's monotonic firmware/control/association/EAPOL state for the exact `wifi diag` id, pair epoch, and generation. Split any identity fields that cannot fit one untruncated 243-byte payload into exact ordered same-identity rows, keep the essential transaction within the bounded response backlog, and fail closed on missing, malformed, reordered, clipped, cross-command, cross-pair, cross-generation, replacement, recovery, or later-attempt evidence. These rows are passive acceptance receipts only and add no driver issue, notification, wake, retry, poll, deadline, recovery, scheduling, or owner authority.
  - scripts/pi4_serial_reboot.py + focused helper fixtures — on the settled WiFi diagnostics path, request `smp activity` before fresh `nettest`/terminal `netstats` and `wifi diag` so the retained ordered old-good prefix precedes its fresh network/DPC tail in the durable serial transcript. Preserve the existing settled-supervisor and DHCP barriers, command grammar, error handling, redaction, and bounded command synchronization; this host-only ordering carries no target or acceptance authority and cannot make missing proof pass.
  - Diagnostic RX timing split (`m26b-wifi-join-owner-forensic-decision`) — version the fixed 128-byte RX batch to v3 while preserving `source_cntvct_lo: [u32; 8]` at bytes 88-119 and the sequence-last parent commit at byte 124. Bytes 120-123 become one sequence-covered `first_data_stage_deltas_q11` for the first populated `CHANNEL_DATA` entry: low `u16` is the floor of DPC source to successful durable private-queue commit and high `u16` is the floor of that queue commit to the final precommit evidence-word sample, both in 2^11-CNTVCT-tick units (about 37.9 us). Values `0x0000..=0xfffe` are valid, including raw zero; `0xffff` is saturated/UNKNOWN. The runtime writes zero when no DATA entry exists, but the passive word is excluded from batch validity and authority identity. If two otherwise exact stable samples differ only in that word, root accepts the batch with both halves degraded to `0xffff`; the mismatch cannot reject traffic or authorize recovery. Root retains a private stable-copy CNTVCT word and, only after exact paired response acceptance, reports the existing source-to-acceptance interval plus `rxsplit_q11a` (`s2q`, `q2p`) and `rxsplit_q11b` (`p2r`, `r2a`, `sat`, `inv`, and one same-sample slow tuple). `p2r` is the exact source-to-copy residual after subtracting both independently floored Q11 stages, so it includes less than 4,096 ticks of combined quantization remainder (about 75.9 us at 54 MHz). This evidence adds no wake, poll, timer, owner, lane, issue, retry, deadline, recovery, or scheduling authority.
  - J4 per-child owner-seam decision (`m26b-wifi-join-owner-forensic-decision`) — exact image `aabb9b39ecc4` reached first-pair Gate 8 and DHCP with no live-ring poison, RX-queue poison, `recovery_required`, sticky pair restart, or priority-lease poison; its stale compatibility client snapshot did not poison or govern the healthy live owner. Raw TCP still missed acceptance. Its valid worst same-sample split was 33.841 ms total: `s2q=27.154 ms` (80.2%), `q2p=5.309 ms`, `p2r=1.228 ms`, and `r2a=0.148 ms`. This is J4: association and the J1/J2/J3 ownership chain are complete, while the first causal performance seam remains inside DPC source admission to durable private-RX queue commit. Add one passive two-region trace that follows the same immutable physical epoch, DPC event, child sequence, descriptor fingerprint, action, I/O phase, and selected engine. `DriverRuntimeSdioChildTimingMailbox` occupies one cache-isolated line in the SDIO owner ring at offset 1,920, wholly within bytes 1,920-1,983: the owner fault record ends at 1,912, the clock snapshot begins at 1,984, and the shared payload starts at 4,096. CYW43 stages the immutable child identity and publication CNTVCT before the normal sequence-last command handoff; SDIO validates and preserves that body, records exact owner intake, physical issue, and joined-terminal CNTVCT, and commits the child sequence last before the normal completion. CYW43 then stable-reads the exact committed mailbox through its mapped owner ring and adds the acceptance CNTVCT to one 28-byte entry. `DriverRuntimeCyw43DpcChildTimingRecord` is an exact 512-byte CYW43/root record with at most sixteen entries beginning at shared offset 49,472, immediately after the 49,344-49,471 bus-episode record and ending at 49,984, before the RX-batch-region end at 53,248. Root reads only the joined CYW43 trace and never writes either record. Missing, coalesced, or repeated hints change no authority or progress. and bounded to the compact scalar header plus active payload; commit mismatch and
````

````text
child-to-root pages only. Target disassembly must show bounded small page-helper

after containment/quarantine. Those failures invalidate the multi-material child

The canonical v6 run used root ELF SHA-256
`0059fd675b476106888d6ca62c8bba21f9b340b9aa607e000fbf96997fd29900`.
Its healthy child remained blocked on notification and no Recovery ran, but

no-op lower path, not qualification.

Historical QEMU v7–v10 results remain failures. V7 exhausted the first Operator during serial poll/flush; v8 still composed multiple output records; v9 exhausted the initial post-bind refill before copying any FIFO bytes, so it did not prove copy cost or disprove the one-record bound. V10 emitted marker/prompt after the activation Yield but exhausted a fresh Runtime visit; its v3 child separately composed a successor on the residual SC. Complete child containment and healthy NineDoor were observed. These results preserve the activation, serial/output, Network and Recovery distinctions and confer no qualification. Exact ELF/CPIO identities, fault registers and traces are retained in the milestone's historical evidence archive.

A fresh four-core GICv3 QEMU run must pass authentication and the exact
two-session generation-fence canary before separate fresh-boot standard-fault
containment and budget-exhaustion/postponement liveness-isolation injection, the
full canonical `.coh` batch, Hive Gateway pressure
benchmarks, or complete host-tool validation can begin. On one boot, session 1
must authenticate, attach, begin a streamed response, and close with a control
still staged or retained; session 2 must then authenticate and attach. The old
control must complete once without child fault or any byte entering session 2,
and session 2 must receive its own bounded ACK/TAIL/END response while the sole
listener remains live. After root-control
binds its generated SC and timeout endpoint, one universal activation-seam
yield must sacrifice the initial remainder and wait for the next replenishment
before any containment probe or first Operator. Root-control then cycles exclusive
Operator/Dispatch, Runtime/IPC, and Network phases across the sole recurring outer
yield with the exact ownership and adjacency defined above; buffered TCP commands
never execute in the NIC turn, runtime IPC never shares that turn, and physical
input/output remains bounded and live. Each isolated QEMU Runtime visit selects
exactly one persistent unit in the order Worker -> ControlEndpoint ->
BootstrapDrain -> StreamFlush -> RebootTail, commits the successor before the
compact isolated-VirtIO Runtime prelude, and returns to the sole outer yield.
That prelude reads the HAL timebase and performs one timer poll. An observed
tick updates `now_ms`, increments the timer metric, publishes HAL timebase, and
runs the existing conditional timer trace; without a tick, `now_ms` takes the
read timebase. It then reconciles CYW43 network-ready HDMI state and must not
execute the generic Runtime-without-control tail. Worker consumes at
most one pending mailbox operation or checks one retained
Heartbeat/GPU/LoRA role slot; ControlEndpoint performs at most one poll and its
immediate forward; BootstrapDrain takes exactly one staged `Option`; RebootTail
owns its visit. The MCS fault poll is absent from the cursor. StreamFlush uses distinct
visits for its terminal sequence: one visit emits one retained final line and
returns; the next no-line visit performs cursor/bandwidth finalization only and
returns; the third visit emits END only and returns. Every earlier line likewise
uses its own visit. Legacy Pi/non-VirtIO Runtime retains its existing
48-line/16-KiB bound. Each isolated VirtIO Operator must create
one generated `64`-byte serial-I/O credit, share it across every root-context
RX poll and TX flush, and retain unfinished bytes after exhaustion. Entry-time
TX backlog reserves exactly `32` bytes for TX and caps RX at `32`; without
entry backlog RX may consume all `64`. Tests must reject helper-local credit
reset, total service above `64`, pending output starvation under sustained RX,
or application of this cut to linked-runtime/Pi paths. With a nonzero generated
VirtIO serial bound, the Operator must attempt at most one retained output
record and preserve every later FIFO and response-tail record for a later
Operator. Tests must reject a second record attempt or helper-local reset of
that admission; Pi and non-VirtIO turns must retain the existing two-record
attempt limit and prior phasing. Each isolated Network visit must attempt
exactly one internal unit, including a no-op. The persistent lower cursor must
cycle ObserveChild -> StageOutput -> Disconnect -> Ingress -> ServiceTick ->
ObserveChild. Every no-op advances; every successful unit that signals the child
forces the next lower attempt to ObserveChild. A pending compact normal-success
diagnostic emits first, at most once on a non-publish visit, and returns.
Otherwise retained TX preempts the lower cursor, performs no more than two
reclaim checks, atomically attempts at most one TX plus any required notify, and
returns on success or backpressure. Both preemptions must preserve the exact
lower cursor and forced-observe state. The bounded gate must sample successful
attempt sequences 0 through 63 and every 64th eligible success thereafter,
never every post-window TX, while counters remain continuous. ObserveChild may
retain egress but must return without TX. Tests must prove Observe -> TX ->
DeferredDiagnostic before resuming the preserved lower state for a sampled
success, while anomalies remain immediate.
Tests must reject post-publication buffer mutation, completion waiting, duplicate
publication, premature head reuse, or dropped retained egress.
The V18 child must recheck its badge and publication-credit gates between
logical units in retained-first order: completion publication, service-event
publication, egress publication, service-poll continuation, new ingress, then
new control. Exact eligible private `PollService`, `IngestPacket`, or
`ApplyControl` work uses `seL4_Poll`; idle or publication-uncredited work calls
`seL4_Wait` directly with no ordinary `seL4_Yield`. Natural postponement, not a
terminal timeout handler, bounds SC exhaustion. Ready and ordinary wakes grant no credit;
internal work preserves it, and one Publish consumes it before mutation.
Stable empty packet/control hints must retire before their one separate service
cycle so Poll cannot self-loop. Root sets ACK debt only after valid copy, clears
it before post-retention signal, and forces ObserveChild; duplicate/late ACK
fails closed. Revoke parks without publication; graceful shutdown waits for
credit, retires terminal debt without ACK, and starts bounded containment.
Within retained `ChildTurnUnit::PollService`, tests must prove the private
`StackIngress -> StackEgress -> Session` cursor commits before dispatch,
`StackIngress` executes exactly one `poll_ingress_single`, `StackEgress`
executes one `poll_egress`, and both return `Continuation`; `Session` owns the
connection/session RX, tick, TX, close, and relisten work. Any nonzero receive
or committed wire frame returns `Continuation`; only zero receive plus no
commit returns `Complete`, and the kernel clears `service_pending` only after
that `Complete`.
Session output must call `send_slice` only when the socket reports both
`can_send()` and complete-frame capacity. Free capacity in `FinWait2` must
retain the complete output without commit, `OutputDrained`, or error; peer
close must reach exactly one `Disconnected`, clear the ended connection, and
relisten. Established-state complete sends remain covered, and no other
Session backpressure or error is converted to continuation.
Every committed control must retain its nonzero connection identity through
the child reader. Kind and payload validation precede identity disposition.
A well-formed stale identity must advance the exact accepted sequence and
publish `ControlCompleted` without output, `OutputDrained`, or fault; zero-id,
malformed, and matching-current pre-authentication errors remain fail-closed.
After connection 1 ends and connection 2 authenticates, a delayed connection-1
`SendLine` must leave connection-2 output byte-for-byte unchanged, while a
connection-2 control remains applicable. Root must retain the one-slot control
owner across `Disconnected` until its exact completion, and an unauthenticated
isolated adapter must return backpressure so the root stream cursor cannot
advance before lifecycle cleanup.
An error or coalesced badge cannot skip the one-unit/refill Yield boundary or
falsely complete PollService. Retained publication must force a blocking Wait
until explicit ACK credit; eligible internal work may Poll after Yield. The isolated QEMU
VirtIO Operator must also prove that the first bounded priority pass returns
before its repeated tail when the exact serviceable-work snapshot is empty,
while any real serial/local-seat/authenticated-line/output/barrier/reboot or
attached display/attach work prevents that cut. Pi/non-VirtIO behavior must not
use the v12 empty-Operator cut.

Before every phase, console-network containment has conditional-probe precedence
over NineDoor; any attempted containment owns one complete Recovery refill,
advances at most one material unit in fixed owner-local order, persists its
cursor, performs zero ordinary phase work, preserves that phase plus both

without pump fallthrough.
Simultaneous faults require every console unit before any NineDoor unit, with
one outer yield per material unit and no interleaved ordinary turn.

state while fencing NIC polls in Network rather than combining Operator and

listener or root-owned physical NIC path appears. The v5 candidate remains
falsified; the v6 root candidate is falsified by the exact composed no-op
Network turn; the v7 root candidate is falsified by the exact first-Operator
serial-flush timeout with its lifecycle marker still queued; the v8 root

exhaustion before its first prospective FIFO move copied any byte; and the v10
root plus v3 child candidates are falsified by the exact fresh-Runtime and
residual-child-SC evidence above. The exact v11 root and v4 child artifacts are
falsified by the `PollService` and post-empty-Operator outer-Yield timeouts
above, despite complete containment. Historical QEMU failure sequence (full identities and traces in the milestone evidence archive): v12/v5 failed authentication after the generic Runtime prelude composed with empty BootstrapDrain; its dirty source differs from the later HEAD. V13/v5 failed ObserveChild in the generic Network adapter, followed by root-fault terminal-path timeout; the child remained healthy. V16 still paid generic EventPump/Operator frames and then root-fault timed out before suspension. V17 executed the compact path but retained combined serial RX/TX work. V18 exhausted StageOutput's Network prelude before any child signal. V19 exhausted the post-leaf counter/NETDIAG/NineDoor aggregate after a no-op Disconnect. These were non-claiming failures; none qualifies child v5 or later artifacts.

V20 retains one compact
diagnostic observation after each timer-plus-one-NIC visit and consumes it in
the next Network visit through NETDIAG only, without timer or NIC work.

The exact immutable v20 root ELF
`ed5cb9f587d0d63e6121f8b00b083e68f5a0a7dd23dd6d2bbf0c899e1e85e80f`
and CPIO
`ca2a52038eb0814a17c8609f03bec32ff357fdd524edee3e7080ac69ceb7823b`
reached the root marker and prompt but then entered root-emergency fail-stop.
Root-control timeout `.1` was at outer-Yield PC `0xf680c`; successor Operator
and retained NETDIAG `Some` prove the timer-plus-NIC visit completed while the

`Timer -> Nic -> DeferredDiagnostic -> Timer` when diagnostics are featured.

`out/cohesix-v8-stale-control-hvf-qemu10-20260813T090943Z` bound rootserver
SHA-256 `f200ecf56021e5eb712e28ecd3ea290d476552166ba2ce3a6b6b5d959f811910`,

`ed0b2b7ecbf2c9f302a30d087d54fbfe264fbfe6857f1717f94319ae4735b551`,
````

````text
    next action becomes visible, not a NIC, RF, DMA, IRQ, ring, retry, or queue
    defect.

    Advance the fixed-size driver runtime-init shared protocol from v12 to v13.
    Reply-consuming MCS bootstrap calls retain the supervised Call/Reply lane,
    donating the current root activation through the exact first descriptor or
    QEMU trampoline completion; classic bootstrap retains its bounded one-way
    behavior. A genuinely asynchronous generic MCS one-way command uses one
    initial notification prompt. After each bounded pending action, its linked
    runtime commits an exact 24-byte `DROW` record in the auxiliary slot with
    request sequence, command-action fingerprint, sealed runtime identity,
    strictly-next wait slice, and sequence-last commit, then atomically
    `NBSendWait`s the existing slot-12 root-control fan-in and slot-3 local
    notification. Root accepts only the current stable record, current ring,
    and current capability generation, changes only the magic to durable
    `DROA`, then signals that child once. The child resumes only after observing
    the exact acknowledgement and clears it before the next action. Stale or
    coalesced badges, duplicate or skipped slices, wrong identity, torn state,
    a terminal race, or an auxiliary grant/steady/persistent record grant no
    authority and fail closed. CYW43/SDIO keep their existing hard source-yield
    boundary; Serial, GENET, HDMI, USB, and PCIe generic work no longer insert
    an unconditional post-action Yield that forfeits the current refill.

    Preserve every SC budget, period, refill, priority, MCP, WCET, Reply and
    fault-supervisor rule, isolation boundary, queue, owner, retry, recovery,
    containment, quarantine, manifest population, and QEMU selector. The
    driver-runtime manifest producer and its independent pipeline oracle advance
    to v13 in the same change. The remaining host-tool suite,
    `tools/cohesix-py`, `.coh` workloads, public console/TCP/REST grammar,
    `scripts/rest_perf_harness.py`, raw/REST workload arithmetic, and report
    schemas were reviewed and require no change.
    Focused ABI/runtime/root tests and the pinned QEMU 10.1/HVF root-MCS canary
    qualify only a candidate. A fresh exact-image cold/warm WiFi and two GENET
    lifetimes with canonical scripts, raw64, medium/high REST, paired captures,
    diagnostics, and operator-path checks remain the direct performance and
    repeatability discriminator.
  - platform consistency — select the same v6 shared contract, including the
    v4-introduced bounded command batch, complete 256-Worker population, two
    passive-Worker executor-lane architecture, and passive-service locality
    invariant for QEMU and Pi manifests. Pi selects a larger root CNode only
    for its additional driver inventory and future capability headroom; QEMU
    optimization and static Pi composition require no Pi hardware interaction.
  - Pi build compatibility — refresh the repository-managed `pi4_diagnostic` tree from the freshly built SMP+MCS profile, require exact current-profile validation and deterministic immutable-tree composition, use the selected `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` for every Pi runtime payload, and compile direct VirtIO DMA containment only for QEMU/VirtIO profiles. Bind the pre-root-task Pi driver-runtime CPIO into image provenance and copy that exact archive into the SD stage, so `--skip-build` cannot repackage a later QEMU-feature child from the shared Cargo target directory. Retain the exact selected Pi resolved manifest beside the staged image before cleanup restores canonical QEMU generated outputs, and bind the stage-only runtime/DMA proof to that retained file and digest.
  - compatibility — preserve public AUTH/framing/command/ACK/ERR/END behavior, one-slot publication ACK semantics, root policy authority, MCS admission, evidence, and fault containment; advance the selected manifest through schema 1.16: schema 1.15 retains the generated internal Worker execution contract and transport correlation identity, while schema 1.16 seals the root-control fan-in slots and internal console/driver ABI versions. Update compiler, generated truth, host/target NineDoor, focused tests, host tools, Python library, SwarmUI projections, and benchmark compatibility review together. Full host-tool and `tools/cohesix-py` review preserves the public grammar, generated target-profile contract, and host dependencies. It requires bounded host-only compatibility repairs: `cohsh` TCP-only builds no longer compile in the in-process NineDoor/mock/trace implementation, in-process integration tests declare that required feature explicitly, `cas-tool` and SwarmUI integration tests resolve their Cargo-provided binaries at runtime so all-target linting remains portable, and the Python target validator adopts the generated Pi 1/127/128 population. The evidence and benchmark tools additionally derive the complete 265-task QEMU and 272-task Pi temporal seals from generated truth, keep exactly one detailed live exemplar for each executable role, admit target-neutral fresh-Pi proof, and compare provenance-compatible QEMU/Pi throughput and errors without turning QEMU latency into a physical-network gate. Hive Gateway's `/v1/meta/status` projection adds required normalized configured-backend `target_host` and `target_port` fields so fresh-Pi evidence binds the live gateway to the exact Pi console endpoint; it does not expose the REST bind endpoint or create target proof for host-model mode. `coh`, `cohsh`, `coh-status`, SwarmUI, `gpu-bridge-host`, `host-ticket-agent`, `host-sidecar-bridge`, `sidecar-bus`, `cas-tool`, every `.coh` workload, and `tools/cohesix-py` tolerate or ignore those additive status fields and require no behavior or schema change. Existing QEMU results do not claim Pi performance, and the enlarged Pi declaration requires fresh exact-image physical evidence. The original manifest/build integration performed no Pi hardware interaction; later exact-image bullets bind their own fresh Pi evidence and still require a new exact-image boot after each source candidate.
````

## m26e-qemu-performance-result

Excerpt SHA-256: `b597f254aea84b70d82964050bf269b56d3a4116ba3dca1b777b10edb62225f7`.

````text
Result (2026-08-25): the v152 same-source performance candidate passed fresh
MEDIUM and HIGH boots on macOS 26.6.2/QEMU 10.1.0 HVF and Jetson Ubuntu
24.04/QEMU 10.1.0 KVM. Both hosts sealed all 265 fault sources, published
256/256 structured Worker READY records, completed authenticated NineDoor,
console, GPU, and LoRA work, and recorded no unexpected standard fault, MCS
timeout, receipt loss, reconnect, or queue runaway. Mac sustained 628.5 and
673.825 successful operations/s; Jetson sustained 93.142 and 100.65. Relative
to the first 37-Worker same-harness Mac baseline, successful MEDIUM/HIGH
throughput improved 14.3x/14.2x while the isolated population grew 6.9x. At
HIGH, the guest used the complete bounded 4096-unit service quantum: aggregate
drainage was 0.994 on Mac and 0.844 on Jetson, with zero flight-recorder
`YIELD`, `TIMEOUT`, or `FAULT` exits. Mac QEMU averaged approximately one host
CPU, so the remaining material ceiling is the deliberately single
authenticated target-control stream rather than artificial MCS fragmentation.
Exact metrics, typed retained-state refusals, commands, identities, and
artifact paths are qualified in `docs/BENCHMARKS.md`; this is QEMU performance
evidence and not Pi hardware acceptance.
````

## m27c-agent-adoption-validation

Excerpt SHA-256: `780b8661130792a24d414b454caae40e52d3fb2f36b890b5defb4e5e6ee30bc7`.

````text
### Agent adoption validation — 19 September 2026 <a id="m27c-agent-adoption-validation"></a>

#### Design and authority

The owner explicitly added this documentation-only task to 27c on 19 September.
It is independent of concurrent CUDA recipe development and does not close the
recipe milestone or activate 27d/27e. Validation used an isolated checkout of
`3452248de`; unrelated working-tree implementation and evidence were excluded.

Three job-oriented skills cover inspection, governed GPU outcomes and evidence.
Python belongs inside those jobs; Jetson and PEFT remain conditional. Each skill
is standalone with no scripts, assets or installation-specific metadata. The
root map and README expose them. Absolute Markdown source links survive copying
a skill folder; instructions require matching local references or pinning the
URL to the installed source revision. No source-only command is promised in an
older release. Release discovery uses actual artifact metadata rather than a
hard-coded release label.

Format follows the [Agent Skills specification](https://agentskills.io/specification)
and current [llms.txt convention](https://llmstxt.org/), consulted on this date.
The skills are operating guidance; AGENTS.md remains the contributor charter.

#### Executed checks

All final checks below passed. Logs and temporary probes were kept under ignored
`out/agent-adoption/` in the validation checkout; no one-off helper was added to
tracked `scripts/`.

- `cargo build -p coh -p cohsh`: native macOS ARM64 default-feature host binaries
  for command checks; not a release/FUSE/GPU-feature or target qualification build.
- `scripts/check-generated.sh`: source/generated contracts match.
- `scripts/ci/check_test_plan.sh`: catalog, documentation and cross-file integrity.
- `python3 scripts/ci/check_implementation_surfaces.py --inventory configs/generated/implementation_surface_inventory.json`.
- `python3 scripts/ci/check_host_integration_inventory.py --matrix-only --state-dir out/agent-adoption/inventory`:
  metadata only; 47 surfaces, 29 dependencies, 9 playbooks, 6 use cases. The first
  invocation omitted required `--state-dir`; the corrected command passed.
- `scripts/ci/check_mermaid_github.sh --markdown-list out/agent-adoption/markdown-list.txt`
  and `git diff --check`.
- Skill-creator `quick_validate.py` for all three skills; independent frontmatter,
  directory/name, description length, file-header and under-500-line checks.
- All new map/skill links resolved to local candidate files; existing remote
  canonical targets returned HTTP 200. New unpublished links were checked locally
  before publication. Links use no author-home or source-relative parent paths.
  Shell fences passed `bash -n`; Python snippets parsed successfully.
- Exact `cohsh --transport mock --role queen` tour: root listing, boot, scheduler
  and lease reads, then quit. All returned OK with simulated records.
- Exact temporary-directory Python `MockBackend` snippet, run from outside the
  source checkout with the candidate SDK: synthetic MockGPU returned; temporary
  storage removed. No target selected via ambient environment.
- `coh evidence pack --mock --out DIR`, `inspect --input DIR --json`,
  `evidence timeline --input DIR`, and `diff --left DIR --right DIR`: successful
  model pack, offline inspection, four derived timeline/case files and same-pack
  comparison. `coh gpu --mock list` and `status --gpu GPU-0` also passed.
- `coh providers`, `coh plan jetson-traffic-safety`, and `coh explain jetson-traffic-safety`:
  read-only generated declarations, explicitly not production use-case acceptance.
- Exact live-read curl and Python snippets exercised against a localhost HTTP
  fixture: required request-auth/delegated headers, GET paths and 1024-byte CAT
  query verified. This tests client construction, not real gateway authorization.
- CLI help/parser plus source review verified every documented command family,
  option order and outcome path. Live GPU submission and signed verification
  were not executed; no deployment request, enrolled trust or live graph was
  supplied for this task. Mutations were not sent to a target.

#### Fresh-context trials

An independent agent began with only `llms.txt`, task prompts and permission to
resolve candidate links locally. It executed the exact mock Python example,
mock cohsh tour and CLI help checks. Other scenarios were static task trials:

| Request | Observed routing and decision |
| --- | --- |
| Inspect configured Queen | Select inspection, preserve sole gateway owner, require identity and scoped read credentials; distinguish connected mock from live console. |
| Python without target | Explicit temporary MockBackend, model-labelled synthetic output; executed. |
| GPU submit timed out | Preserve original identity, reconcile journal/native/evidence, no blind resubmit or implicit recovery. |
| Sealed partial pack / signed failure | Inspect partial coverage, copy before timeline, independent trust; signed failure does not establish requested success. |
| Jetson PEFT activation | Separate local registry, publication, native serving and verified deployment; no complete 27d claim. |
| Standalone training, CUDA installation, unrestricted shell | Route native host tasks away from Cohesix; no widened authority or shell fallback. |
| MCP/A2A | Current REST implementation, future 28g scope; no invented endpoint or server. |

The first trial found missing delegated read credentials and a nonexistent
`/host/tickets/result` path. Both were corrected and independently rereviewed;
recovery now reads bounded `status.snapshot` and `deadletter.snapshot`. That
review also exposed stale read-auth paragraphs and bare curl examples in the
linked canonical guides. The same scoped change aligns those read instructions
with the existing gateway read classifier and Rust/Python header handling.

#### Benefit-led discovery correction — 19 September 2026

The owner reopened this documentation task to replace unexplained terminology
with user benefits and make every target playbook discoverable. `llms.txt` now
leads with useful delegation, shared GPU coordination, reusable CUDA work and
reviewable outcomes; defines Queen and Worker on first use; maps all six use
cases and nine playbooks to plain-language goals; and invites a no-hardware
inspection trial. Catalogue mappings were checked against the generated registry
at `ff646848f`. Format, 26 unique HTTP links, local anchors, generated consistency,
Test Plan integrity and `git diff --check` passed in isolation. This was a static
copy/coverage review, not a new agent-adoption or live-execution trial. No command,
interface, permission, benchmark or implementation changed.

#### Compatibility and limits

Reviewed all eight host tools: coh, cohsh, hive-gateway, gpu-bridge-host,
host-sidecar-bridge, host-ticket-agent, cas-tool and SwarmUI; also cohesix-py,
`rest_perf_harness.py`, benchmark methodology, generated provider/implementation
inventories and packaged release guidance. No command, schema, bound, default,
permission, transport, executor, benchmark workload or report format changes.
The three guides need only read-auth documentation corrections. Other surfaces
require no implementation/fixture change because this task adds entry points to
existing contracts rather than changing those contracts.

No live Queen/GPU/Jetson execution, fresh Pi evidence, target acceptance,
production identity enrollment, external-user adoption study or website deployment
was performed. Signed-verifier positive/negative runtime behavior was reviewed
in implementation and existing tests, not freshly exercised with enrolled graphs.
Link/format checks and mock or HTTP-fixture results cannot establish adoption,
live authorization, native completion, workload success or release acceptance.

````
